FILE: frontend/android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/frontend/KyivAlertWidgetProvider.kt
------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.frontend

import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetManager
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetProvider
import android.content.ComponentName
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.util.Log
import android.view.View
import android.widget.RemoteViews
import java.io.File
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

class KyivAlertWidgetProvider : AppWidgetProvider() {

    override fun onUpdate(context: Context, appWidgetManager: AppWidgetManager, appWidgetIds: IntArray) {
        for (appWidgetId in appWidgetIds) {
            try {
                updateAppWidget(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetId)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Error in onUpdate for widget $appWidgetId", e)
                try {
                    renderFallbackWidget(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetId)
                } catch (t: Throwable) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Error rendering fallback widget in onUpdate", t)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "KyivAlertWidget"
        private const val PREFS_NAME = "kyiv_alert_widget"
        private const val MAX_BITMAP_WIDTH = 440
        private const val MAX_BITMAP_HEIGHT = 300

        fun updateAllWidgets(context: Context) {
            try {
                val appWidgetManager = AppWidgetManager.getInstance(context) ?: return
                val thisWidget = ComponentName(context, KyivAlertWidgetProvider::class.java)
                val appWidgetIds = appWidgetManager.getAppWidgetIds(thisWidget) ?: return
                for (appWidgetId in appWidgetIds) {
                    try {
                        updateAppWidget(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetId)
                    } catch (e: Throwable) {
                        Log.e(TAG, "Error updating widget $appWidgetId", e)
                        try {
                            renderFallbackWidget(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetId)
                        } catch (t: Throwable) {
                            Log.e(TAG, "Error rendering fallback widget in updateAllWidgets", t)
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Error in updateAllWidgets", e)
            }
        }

        fun updateAppWidget(context: Context, appWidgetManager: AppWidgetManager, appWidgetId: Int) {
            try {
                val prefs = try {
                    context.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
                } catch (e: Throwable) {
                    null
                }

                val statusTitle = prefs?.getString("statusTitle", "БЕЗПЕЧНО") ?: "БЕЗПЕЧНО"
                val activeCount = prefs?.getInt("activeCount", 0) ?: 0
                val nearestInfo = prefs?.getString("nearestInfo", "Немає загроз поблизу") ?: "Немає загроз поблизу"
                val lastMsgText = prefs?.getString("lastMsgText", "") ?: ""
                val updatedTime = prefs?.getString("updatedTime", "")?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
                    ?: SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
                val isAlert = prefs?.getBoolean("isAlert", false) ?: false

                val views = RemoteViews(context.packageName, R.layout.kyiv_alert_widget)

                // Setup click intent to launch MainActivity
                val intent = Intent(context, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                    flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
                }
                val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                    context,
                    0,
                    intent,
                    PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
                )
                views.setOnClickPendingIntent(R.id.widget_root, pendingIntent)

                // Background & Border styling
                if (isAlert) {
                    views.setInt(R.id.widget_root, "setBackgroundResource", R.drawable.widget_bg_alert)
                    views.setTextColor(R.id.widget_status_title, Color.parseColor("#FF5252"))
                } else {
                    views.setInt(R.id.widget_root, "setBackgroundResource", R.drawable.widget_bg_normal)
                    views.setTextColor(R.id.widget_status_title, Color.parseColor("#4CAF50"))
                }

                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_status_title, statusTitle)

                // Target Count
                if (activeCount > 0) {
                    views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_threat_count, "• $activeCount цілей")
                    views.setViewVisibility(R.id.widget_threat_count, View.VISIBLE)
                } else {
                    views.setViewVisibility(R.id.widget_threat_count, View.GONE)
                }

                // Nearest Info & ETA
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_nearest_info, nearestInfo)

                // Last message snippet
                if (lastMsgText.isNotBlank()) {
                    views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_last_msg, lastMsgText)
                    views.setViewVisibility(R.id.widget_last_msg, View.VISIBLE)
                } else {
                    views.setViewVisibility(R.id.widget_last_msg, View.GONE)
                }

                // Updated timestamp
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_updated_time, updatedTime)

                // Load Radar Snapshot with strict safety & size bounds
                loadRadarBitmap(context, views)

                appWidgetManager.updateAppWidget(appWidgetId, views)
            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Failed updateAppWidget $appWidgetId, falling back", e)
                renderFallbackWidget(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetId)
            }
        }

        private fun loadRadarBitmap(context: Context, views: RemoteViews) {
            try {
                val imageFile = File(context.filesDir, "radar_snapshot.png")
                if (imageFile.exists() && imageFile.length() > 0) {
                    val options = BitmapFactory.Options().apply {
                        inPreferredConfig = Bitmap.Config.RGB_565
                    }
                    val rawBitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeFile(imageFile.absolutePath, options)
                    if (rawBitmap != null) {
                        // Enforce max dimensions 440x300 px
                        val scaledBitmap = if (rawBitmap.width > MAX_BITMAP_WIDTH || rawBitmap.height > MAX_BITMAP_HEIGHT) {
                            Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(rawBitmap, MAX_BITMAP_WIDTH, MAX_BITMAP_HEIGHT, true)
                        } else {
                            rawBitmap
                        }

                        // Ensure RGB_565 format (< 550 KB) to avoid TransactionTooLargeException
                        val finalBitmap = if (scaledBitmap.config != Bitmap.Config.RGB_565) {
                            try {
                                val rgb565 = Bitmap.createBitmap(scaledBitmap.width, scaledBitmap.height, Bitmap.Config.RGB_565)
                                val canvas = Canvas(rgb565)
                                canvas.drawBitmap(scaledBitmap, 0f, 0f, null)
                                rgb565
                            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                                scaledBitmap
                            }
                        } else {
                            scaledBitmap
                        }

                        views.setImageViewBitmap(R.id.widget_radar_image, finalBitmap)
                        return
                    }
                }

                // Fallback: якщо radar_snapshot.png відсутній або пошкоджений — генеруємо нативний радарний Bitmap 440x300 px
                try {
                    val fallbackBitmap = RadarBitmapRenderer.render(
                        context,
                        emptyList(),
                        emptyList(),
                        false,
                        "БЕЗПЕЧНО",
                        SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
                    )
                    val scaledFallback = if (fallbackBitmap.width > MAX_BITMAP_WIDTH || fallbackBitmap.height > MAX_BITMAP_HEIGHT) {
                        Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(fallbackBitmap, MAX_BITMAP_WIDTH, MAX_BITMAP_HEIGHT, true)
                    } else {
                        fallbackBitmap
                    }
                    val finalFallback = if (scaledFallback.config != Bitmap.Config.RGB_565) {
                        try {
                            val rgb565 = Bitmap.createBitmap(scaledFallback.width, scaledFallback.height, Bitmap.Config.RGB_565)
                            val c = Canvas(rgb565)
                            c.drawBitmap(scaledFallback, 0f, 0f, null)
                            rgb565
                        } catch (t: Throwable) {
                            scaledFallback
                        }
                    } else {
                        scaledFallback
                    }
                    views.setImageViewBitmap(R.id.widget_radar_image, finalFallback)
                    return
                } catch (t: Throwable) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "Fallback RadarBitmapRenderer failed", t)
                }
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                Log.w(TAG, "Error decoding radar snapshot bitmap", t)
            }
            views.setImageViewResource(R.id.widget_radar_image, R.drawable.widget_radar_placeholder)
        }

        fun renderFallbackWidget(context: Context, appWidgetManager: AppWidgetManager, appWidgetId: Int) {
            try {
                val views = RemoteViews(context.packageName, R.layout.kyiv_alert_widget)

                val intent = Intent(context, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
                    flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
                }
                val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
                    context,
                    0,
                    intent,
                    PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
                )
                views.setOnClickPendingIntent(R.id.widget_root, pendingIntent)

                // Чистий базовий темний стан віджета (#12101B з фіолетовою рамкою та статусом "🟢 Моніторинг активний")
                views.setInt(R.id.widget_root, "setBackgroundResource", R.drawable.widget_bg_normal)
                views.setTextColor(R.id.widget_status_title, Color.parseColor("#4CAF50"))
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_status_title, "🟢 Моніторинг активний")

                views.setViewVisibility(R.id.widget_threat_count, View.GONE)
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_nearest_info, "Немає загроз поблизу")
                views.setViewVisibility(R.id.widget_last_msg, View.GONE)

                val currentTime = SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_updated_time, currentTime)

                views.setImageViewResource(R.id.widget_radar_image, R.drawable.widget_radar_placeholder)

                appWidgetManager.updateAppWidget(appWidgetId, views)
            } catch (t: Throwable) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Critical failure in renderFallbackWidget", t)
            }
        }
    }
}


============================================================

FILE: frontend/android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/frontend/LockScreenRadarNotification.kt
------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.frontend

import android.app.Notification
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.os.Build
import java.io.File

object LockScreenRadarNotification {

    const val CHANNEL_ID = "kyiv_alert_radar_lockscreen"
    const val CHANNEL_NAME = "Kyiv Alert Radar"
    const val NOTIFICATION_ID = 2024

    fun buildNotification(context: Context): Notification? {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("kyiv_alert_widget", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val isAlert = prefs.getBoolean("isAlert", false)
        if (!isAlert) return null

        val statusTitle = prefs.getString("statusTitle", "ПОВІТРЯНА ТРИВОГА") ?: "ПОВІТРЯНА ТРИВОГА"
        val activeCount = prefs.getInt("activeCount", 0)
        val nearestInfo = prefs.getString("nearestInfo", "Загроза в повітряному просторі") ?: "Загроза в повітряному просторі"
        val lastMsgText = prefs.getString("lastMsgText", "") ?: ""
        val updatedTime = prefs.getString("updatedTime", "--:--") ?: "--:--"

        val notificationManager = context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
        createChannelIfNeeded(notificationManager)

        val intent = Intent(context, MainActivity::class.java).apply {
            flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP
        }
        val pendingIntent = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            context,
            0,
            intent,
            PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT or PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE
        )

        val title = if (activeCount > 0) {
            "🚨 $statusTitle • $activeCount цілей ($updatedTime)"
        } else {
            "🚨 $statusTitle ($updatedTime)"
        }

        val builder = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            Notification.Builder(context, CHANNEL_ID)
        } else {
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            Notification.Builder(context)
        }

        builder.setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic_launcher)
            .setContentTitle(title)
            .setContentText(nearestInfo)
            .setContentIntent(pendingIntent)
            .setOngoing(true)
            .setOnlyAlertOnce(true)
            .setVisibility(Notification.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC)
            .setCategory(Notification.CATEGORY_STATUS)

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
            builder.setForegroundServiceBehavior(Notification.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_IMMEDIATE)
        }

        // Load native canvas radar snapshot
        val imageFile = File(context.filesDir, "radar_snapshot.png")
        if (imageFile.exists() && imageFile.length() > 0) {
            try {
                val bitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeFile(imageFile.absolutePath)
                if (bitmap != null) {
                    val summary = if (lastMsgText.isNotBlank()) "$nearestInfo\n$lastMsgText" else nearestInfo
                    val style = Notification.BigPictureStyle()
                        .bigPicture(bitmap)
                        .setSummaryText(summary)
                    builder.setStyle(style)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }

        return builder.build()
    }

    fun update(context: Context) {
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("kyiv_alert_widget", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
        val isAlert = prefs.getBoolean("isAlert", false)

        if (isAlert) {
            // Запускаємо або оновлюємо Foreground Service для захисту від вивантаження на One UI / HyperOS
            RadarForegroundService.start(context)
            val notification = buildNotification(context)
            if (notification != null) {
                val notificationManager = context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
                createChannelIfNeeded(notificationManager)
                notificationManager.notify(NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
            }
        } else {
            // Зупиняємо Foreground Service та знімаємо сповіщення
            RadarForegroundService.stop(context)
            val notificationManager = context.getSystemService(Context.NOTIFICATION_SERVICE) as NotificationManager
            notificationManager.cancel(NOTIFICATION_ID)
        }
    }

    fun createChannelIfNeeded(notificationManager: NotificationManager) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            val existing = notificationManager.getNotificationChannel(CHANNEL_ID)
            if (existing == null) {
                val channel = NotificationChannel(
                    CHANNEL_ID,
                    CHANNEL_NAME,
                    NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT
                ).apply {
                    description = "Живий тактичний радар для екрана блокування під час тривоги"
                    lockscreenVisibility = Notification.VISIBILITY_PUBLIC
                    enableLights(true)
                    enableVibration(false)
                }
                notificationManager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
            }
        }
    }
}


============================================================

FILE: frontend/android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/frontend/MainActivity.kt
------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.frontend

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.PowerManager
import android.provider.Settings
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import io.flutter.embedding.android.FlutterActivity
import io.flutter.embedding.engine.FlutterEngine
import io.flutter.plugin.common.MethodChannel
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream

class MainActivity : FlutterActivity() {
    private val CHANNEL = "ua.kyiv.alert.monitor/widget"

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        ensurePermissions()
    }

    private fun ensurePermissions() {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) {
            if (checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                requestPermissions(arrayOf(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS), 1001)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun configureFlutterEngine(flutterEngine: FlutterEngine) {
        super.configureFlutterEngine(flutterEngine)

        MethodChannel(flutterEngine.dartExecutor.binaryMessenger, CHANNEL).setMethodCallHandler { call, result ->
            when (call.method) {
                "updateWidget" -> {
                    try {
                        val statusTitle = call.argument<String>("statusTitle") ?: "БЕЗПЕЧНО"
                        val activeCount = call.argument<Int>("activeCount") ?: 0
                        val nearestInfo = call.argument<String>("nearestInfo") ?: ""
                        val lastMsgText = call.argument<String>("lastMsgText") ?: ""
                        val updatedTime = call.argument<String>("updatedTime") ?: ""
                        val isAlert = call.argument<Boolean>("isAlert") ?: false
                        val radarPng = call.argument<ByteArray>("radarPng")

                        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                        val threats = call.argument<List<Map<String, Any?>>>("threats") ?: emptyList()
                        @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
                        val watchedLocations = call.argument<List<Map<String, Any?>>>("watchedLocations") ?: emptyList()

                        val prefs = getSharedPreferences("kyiv_alert_widget", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
                        prefs.edit()
                            .putString("statusTitle", statusTitle)
                            .putInt("activeCount", activeCount)
                            .putString("nearestInfo", nearestInfo)
                            .putString("lastMsgText", lastMsgText)
                            .putString("updatedTime", updatedTime)
                            .putBoolean("isAlert", isAlert)
                            .apply()

                        // Якщо екран активний і Flutter надіслав готовий скріншот карти — зберігаємо його.
                        // Якщо ж екран вимкнений / спить — рендеримо нативний Canvas через RadarBitmapRenderer.
                        if (radarPng != null && radarPng.isNotEmpty()) {
                            try {
                                val file = File(filesDir, "radar_snapshot.png")
                                val rawBitmap = BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(radarPng, 0, radarPng.size)
                                if (rawBitmap != null) {
                                    val scaledBitmap = if (rawBitmap.width > 440 || rawBitmap.height > 300) {
                                        Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(rawBitmap, 440, 300, true)
                                    } else {
                                        rawBitmap
                                    }
                                    FileOutputStream(file).use { fos ->
                                        scaledBitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.PNG, 90, fos)
                                        fos.flush()
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    FileOutputStream(file).use { fos ->
                                        fos.write(radarPng)
                                        fos.flush()
                                    }
                                }
                            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                                e.printStackTrace()
                            }
                        } else {
                            try {
                                RadarBitmapRenderer.render(
                                    applicationContext,
                                    threats,
                                    watchedLocations,
                                    isAlert,
                                    statusTitle,
                                    updatedTime
                                )
                            } catch (e: Throwable) {
                                e.printStackTrace()
                            }
                        }

                        // Оновлення віджетів на робочому столі
                        KyivAlertWidgetProvider.updateAllWidgets(applicationContext)

                        // Оновлення або запуск Foreground Service зі сповіщенням на Lock Screen
                        LockScreenRadarNotification.update(applicationContext)

                        result.success(true)
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        e.printStackTrace()
                        result.error("UPDATE_ERROR", e.message, null)
                    }
                }
                "requestIgnoreBatteryOptimizations" -> {
                    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
                        val pm = getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE) as PowerManager
                        if (!pm.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(packageName)) {
                            try {
                                val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS).apply {
                                    data = Uri.parse("package:$packageName")
                                    flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK
                                }
                                startActivity(intent)
                                result.success(true)
                                return@setMethodCallHandler
                            } catch (e: Exception) {
                                e.printStackTrace()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    result.success(false)
                }
                "isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations" -> {
                    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.M) {
                        val pm = getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE) as PowerManager
                        result.success(pm.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(packageName))
                    } else {
                        result.success(true)
                    }
                }
                "installApk" -> {
                    try {
                        val filePath = call.argument<String>("filePath")
                        if (filePath != null) {
                            val file = File(filePath)
                            val canonicalFilePath = file.canonicalPath
                            val canonicalCacheDir = cacheDir.canonicalPath
                            if (!canonicalFilePath.startsWith(canonicalCacheDir) || file.name != "update.apk") {
                                result.error("SECURITY_VIOLATION", "Path traversal or invalid file name rejected", null)
                                return@setMethodCallHandler
                            }
                            if (file.exists()) {
                                val fileLen = file.length()
                                if (fileLen < 1024L || fileLen > 157286400L) {
                                    result.error("SECURITY_VIOLATION", "Invalid APK file size: $fileLen", null)
                                    return@setMethodCallHandler
                                }
                                val magic = ByteArray(4)
                                val readBytes = file.inputStream().use { it.read(magic) }
                                val isZip = readBytes == 4 &&
                                    magic[0] == 0x50.toByte() &&
                                    magic[1] == 0x4B.toByte() &&
                                    magic[2] == 0x03.toByte() &&
                                    magic[3] == 0x04.toByte()
                                if (!isZip) {
                                    try { file.delete() } catch (_: Exception) {}
                                    result.error("SECURITY_VIOLATION", "Invalid APK signature", null)
                                    return@setMethodCallHandler
                                }
                                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                                    if (!packageManager.canRequestPackageInstalls()) {
                                        try {
                                            val manageIntent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES).apply {
                                                data = Uri.parse("package:$packageName")
                                                flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK
                                            }
                                            startActivity(manageIntent)
                                        } catch (se: Exception) {
                                            se.printStackTrace()
                                        }
                                        result.error("PERMISSION_REQUIRED", "Потрібно дозволити 'Встановлювати невідомі додатки'", null)
                                        return@setMethodCallHandler
                                    }
                                }
                                val apkUri = androidx.core.content.FileProvider.getUriForFile(
                                    applicationContext,
                                    "${applicationContext.packageName}.fileprovider",
                                    file
                                )
                                val installIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                                    setDataAndType(apkUri, "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
                                    flags = Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK
                                }
                                startActivity(installIntent)
                                result.success(true)
                            } else {
                                result.error("FILE_NOT_FOUND", "APK file not found at $filePath", null)
                            }
                        } else {
                            result.error("INVALID_ARGUMENT", "File path is null", null)
                        }
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        e.printStackTrace()
                        result.error("INSTALL_ERROR", e.message, null)
                    }
                }
                "canRequestPackageInstalls" -> {
                    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                        result.success(packageManager.canRequestPackageInstalls())
                    } else {
                        result.success(true)
                    }
                }
                "openUnknownAppSourcesSettings" -> {
                    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                        try {
                            val intent = Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES).apply {
                                data = Uri.parse("package:$packageName")
                                flags = Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK
                            }
                            startActivity(intent)
                            result.success(true)
                        } catch (e: Exception) {
                            result.error("SETTINGS_ERROR", e.message, null)
                        }
                    } else {
                        result.success(true)
                    }
                }
                "getCacheDir" -> {
                    result.success(cacheDir.absolutePath)
                }
                "getFilesDir" -> {
                    result.success(filesDir.absolutePath)
                }
                "ensureAndroidPermissions" -> {
                    ensurePermissions()
                    result.success(true)
                }
                else -> {
                    result.notImplemented()
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: frontend/android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/frontend/RadarBitmapRenderer.kt
------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.frontend

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.*
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import kotlin.math.*

object RadarBitmapRenderer {

    private const val WIDTH = 800
    private const val HEIGHT = 420
    private const val CENTER_LAT = 50.4501
    private const val CENTER_LON = 30.5234

    // 1 deg lat ~ 111,139 meters. Aspect scale for 50.45 deg latitude
    private const val SCALE_Y = HEIGHT / 1.75f
    private val COS_LAT = cos(Math.toRadians(CENTER_LAT)).toFloat()
    private val SCALE_X = SCALE_Y / COS_LAT

    private fun project(lat: Double, lon: Double): Pair<Float, Float> {
        val px = (WIDTH / 2f) + ((lon - CENTER_LON) * SCALE_X).toFloat()
        val py = (HEIGHT / 2f) - ((lat - CENTER_LAT) * SCALE_Y).toFloat()
        return Pair(px, py)
    }

    private fun metersToPx(meters: Double): Float {
        return (meters / 111139.0 * SCALE_Y).toFloat()
    }

    fun render(
        context: Context,
        threats: List<Map<String, Any?>>,
        watchedLocations: List<Map<String, Any?>>,
        isAlert: Boolean,
        statusTitle: String,
        updatedTime: String
    ): Bitmap {
        val bitmap = Bitmap.createBitmap(WIDTH, HEIGHT, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        val canvas = Canvas(bitmap)

        // 1. Dark tactical radar background
        canvas.drawColor(Color.parseColor("#0C0E14"))

        val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)

        // Subtle background grid lines (every 60 px)
        paint.color = Color.parseColor("#141724")
        paint.strokeWidth = 1f
        paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        for (x in 0..WIDTH step 60) {
            canvas.drawLine(x.toFloat(), 0f, x.toFloat(), HEIGHT.toFloat(), paint)
        }
        for (y in 0..HEIGHT step 60) {
            canvas.drawLine(0f, y.toFloat(), WIDTH.toFloat(), y.toFloat(), paint)
        }

        // Center crosshairs through Kyiv
        val (kX, kY) = project(CENTER_LAT, CENTER_LON)
        paint.color = Color.parseColor("#1F2538")
        paint.strokeWidth = 1.5f
        canvas.drawLine(kX, 0f, kX, HEIGHT.toFloat(), paint)
        canvas.drawLine(0f, kY, WIDTH.toFloat(), kY, paint)

        // 2. Concentric radar distance rings
        val ringsMeters = listOf(15000.0, 35000.0, 65000.0, 95000.0)
        val ringLabels = listOf("15 км", "35 км", "65 км", "95 км")
        paint.color = Color.parseColor("#262E45")
        paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        paint.strokeWidth = 1.5f
        paint.pathEffect = DashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(6f, 6f), 0f)

        val textPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
            color = Color.parseColor("#485375")
            textSize = 12f
            typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.MONOSPACE, Typeface.NORMAL)
        }

        for (i in ringsMeters.indices) {
            val rPx = metersToPx(ringsMeters[i])
            canvas.drawCircle(kX, kY, rPx, paint)
            canvas.drawText(ringLabels[i], kX + rPx + 4f, kY - 4f, textPaint)
        }
        paint.pathEffect = null // reset dash

        // 3. Reference cities & towns in Kyiv Oblast
        val keyTowns = listOf(
            Triple(50.5113, 30.7903, "Бровари"),
            Triple(50.3522, 30.9547, "Бориспіль"),
            Triple(50.1778, 30.3178, "Васильків"),
            Triple(49.7989, 30.1153, "Біла Церква"),
            Triple(50.5186, 30.2394, "Ірпінь"),
            Triple(50.5847, 30.4903, "Вишгород"),
            Triple(50.0656, 29.9194, "Фастів"),
            Triple(50.1283, 30.6558, "Обухів")
        )

        paint.style = Paint.Style.FILL
        for (town in keyTowns) {
            val (tx, ty) = project(town.first, town.second)
            if (tx in 0f..WIDTH.toFloat() && ty in 0f..HEIGHT.toFloat()) {
                paint.color = Color.parseColor("#384260")
                canvas.drawCircle(tx, ty, 3.5f, paint)

                textPaint.color = Color.parseColor("#5A688C")
                textPaint.textSize = 12f
                textPaint.typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT
                canvas.drawText(town.third, tx + 6f, ty + 4f, textPaint)
            }
        }

        // Kyiv center marker
        paint.color = Color.parseColor("#4CAF50")
        canvas.drawCircle(kX, kY, 6f, paint)
        paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        paint.strokeWidth = 2f
        paint.color = Color.parseColor("#81C784")
        canvas.drawCircle(kX, kY, 11f, paint)

        val kyivTextPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
            color = Color.WHITE
            textSize = 16f
            typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
            setShadowLayer(4f, 0f, 0f, Color.BLACK)
        }
        canvas.drawText("КИЇВ", kX + 14f, kY + 5f, kyivTextPaint)

        // 4. Watched Locations (User Safety Zones)
        for (loc in watchedLocations) {
            val enabled = loc["enabled"] as? Boolean ?: true
            if (!enabled) continue

            val lat = (loc["lat"] as? Number)?.toDouble() ?: continue
            val lon = (loc["lon"] as? Number)?.toDouble() ?: continue
            val radiusMeters = (loc["radiusMeters"] as? Number)?.toDouble() ?: 4000.0
            val name = (loc["name"] as? String) ?: "Дім"

            val (lx, ly) = project(lat, lon)
            val rPx = metersToPx(radiusMeters)

            // Zone Fill
            paint.style = Paint.Style.FILL
            paint.color = Color.argb(45, 124, 77, 255)
            canvas.drawCircle(lx, ly, rPx, paint)

            // Zone Stroke
            paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
            paint.strokeWidth = 2f
            paint.color = Color.argb(180, 156, 39, 176)
            canvas.drawCircle(lx, ly, rPx, paint)

            // Center Pin
            paint.style = Paint.Style.FILL
            paint.color = Color.parseColor("#E040FB")
            canvas.drawCircle(lx, ly, 4f, paint)

            val locTextPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                color = Color.parseColor("#CE93D8")
                textSize = 13f
                typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                setShadowLayer(3f, 0f, 0f, Color.BLACK)
            }
            canvas.drawText("🏠 $name", lx + 8f, ly + 4f, locTextPaint)
        }

        // 5. Active Threats (Trajectories, Velocity Arrows, Markers)
        val linePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
            style = Paint.Style.STROKE
            strokeWidth = 3.5f
            pathEffect = DashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(10f, 6f), 0f)
        }

        val threatTextPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
            textSize = 14f
            typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
            setShadowLayer(5f, 0f, 0f, Color.BLACK)
        }

        for (threat in threats) {
            val type = (threat["type"] as? String) ?: "shahed"
            val label = (threat["label"] as? String) ?: "Ціль"
            val route = (threat["route"] as? String) ?: ""
            val isDescending = threat["isDescending"] as? Boolean ?: false

            val isMissile = type.contains("missile") || type == "kab"
            val threatColor = when {
                type == "missile_hypersonic" -> Color.parseColor("#D500F9")
                type == "missile_ballistic" -> Color.parseColor("#FF1744")
                type.contains("missile") -> Color.parseColor("#FF5252")
                type == "kab" -> Color.parseColor("#FFD600")
                type == "shahed_jet" -> Color.parseColor("#FF4081")
                else -> Color.parseColor("#FF9100") // shahed
            }

            linePaint.color = Color.argb(180, Color.red(threatColor), Color.green(threatColor), Color.blue(threatColor))

            @Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
            val pathList = threat["path"] as? List<Map<String, Any?>> ?: emptyList()
            if (pathList.isEmpty()) continue

            val projectedPoints = pathList.mapNotNull { p ->
                val pLat = (p["lat"] as? Number)?.toDouble()
                val pLon = (p["lon"] as? Number)?.toDouble()
                if (pLat != null && pLon != null) project(pLat, pLon) else null
            }

            if (projectedPoints.size >= 2) {
                val trajectoryPath = Path().apply {
                    moveTo(projectedPoints.first().first, projectedPoints.first().second)
                    for (i in 1 until projectedPoints.size) {
                        lineTo(projectedPoints[i].first, projectedPoints[i].second)
                    }
                }
                canvas.drawPath(trajectoryPath, linePaint)
            }

            // Current target marker (last point)
            val currentPt = projectedPoints.lastOrNull() ?: continue
            val (cx, cy) = currentPt

            // Outer pulse circle
            paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
            paint.strokeWidth = 2.5f
            paint.color = threatColor
            canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, 12f, paint)

            // Inner solid core
            paint.style = Paint.Style.FILL
            paint.color = Color.WHITE
            canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, 5f, paint)

            // Velocity Vector leader line
            if (projectedPoints.size >= 2) {
                val prevPt = projectedPoints[projectedPoints.size - 2]
                val dx = cx - prevPt.first
                val dy = cy - prevPt.second
                val dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
                if (dist > 1f) {
                    val nx = dx / dist
                    val ny = dy / dist
                    val arrowLen = 30f
                    val ax = cx + nx * arrowLen
                    val ay = cy + ny * arrowLen

                    paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                    paint.strokeWidth = 2.5f
                    paint.color = threatColor
                    canvas.drawLine(cx, cy, ax, ay, paint)
                    canvas.drawCircle(ax, ay, 3f, paint)
                }
            }

            // Threat Label Text
            threatTextPaint.color = threatColor
            val fullLabel = if (route.isNotBlank()) "$label → $route" else label
            val finalLabel = if (isDescending) "$fullLabel ↓" else fullLabel
            canvas.drawText(finalLabel, cx + 15f, cy - 6f, threatTextPaint)
        }

        // 6. Top HUD Header: Status Badge & Live Clock
        val hudPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
        val badgeBgColor = if (isAlert) Color.parseColor("#B71C1C") else Color.parseColor("#1B5E20")
        val badgeBorderColor = if (isAlert) Color.parseColor("#FF1744") else Color.parseColor("#4CAF50")

        // Badge pill rect
        val badgeRect = RectF(16f, 16f, 240f, 48f)
        hudPaint.style = Paint.Style.FILL
        hudPaint.color = badgeBgColor
        canvas.drawRoundRect(badgeRect, 16f, 16f, hudPaint)

        hudPaint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        hudPaint.strokeWidth = 1.5f
        hudPaint.color = badgeBorderColor
        canvas.drawRoundRect(badgeRect, 16f, 16f, hudPaint)

        val badgeTextPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
            color = Color.WHITE
            textSize = 14f
            typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
        }
        val badgeIcon = if (isAlert) "🚨 " else "🟢 "
        canvas.drawText("$badgeIcon$statusTitle", 28f, 37f, badgeTextPaint)

        // Top-Right Clock & Sector
        val clockTextPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
            color = Color.parseColor("#A0AEC0")
            textSize = 13f
            typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.MONOSPACE, Typeface.BOLD)
            textAlign = Paint.Align.RIGHT
        }
        canvas.drawText("КИЇВСЬКИЙ СЕКТОР • $updatedTime", (WIDTH - 16).toFloat(), 36f, clockTextPaint)

        // Bottom HUD Branding
        val footerPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
            color = Color.parseColor("#3D4866")
            textSize = 11f
            typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.MONOSPACE, Typeface.NORMAL)
        }
        canvas.drawText("KYIV AIR DEFENSE TACTICAL RADAR", 16f, (HEIGHT - 16).toFloat(), footerPaint)
        canvas.drawText("МАСШТАБ 1:65 KM", (WIDTH - 130).toFloat(), (HEIGHT - 16).toFloat(), footerPaint)

        // Save Bitmap to filesDir/radar_snapshot.png (scaled to max 440x300 px in RGB_565 for widget Binder IPC safety)
        try {
            val file = File(context.filesDir, "radar_snapshot.png")
            val scaledBitmap = if (bitmap.width > 440 || bitmap.height > 300) {
                Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(bitmap, 440, 300, true)
            } else {
                bitmap
            }
            val rgb565 = if (scaledBitmap.config != Bitmap.Config.RGB_565) {
                try {
                    val converted = Bitmap.createBitmap(scaledBitmap.width, scaledBitmap.height, Bitmap.Config.RGB_565)
                    val c = Canvas(converted)
                    c.drawBitmap(scaledBitmap, 0f, 0f, null)
                    converted
                } catch (t: Throwable) {
                    scaledBitmap
                }
            } else {
                scaledBitmap
            }
            FileOutputStream(file).use { fos ->
                rgb565.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.PNG, 90, fos)
                fos.flush()
            }
        } catch (e: Throwable) {
            e.printStackTrace()
        }

        return bitmap
    }
}

FILE: frontend/android/app/src/main/kotlin/com/example/frontend/RadarForegroundService.kt
------------------------------------------------------------
package com.example.frontend

import android.app.Service
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.ServiceInfo
import android.os.Build
import android.os.IBinder

class RadarForegroundService : Service() {

    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder? = null

    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
        val action = intent?.action
        if (action == ACTION_STOP) {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {
                stopForeground(STOP_FOREGROUND_REMOVE)
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                stopForeground(true)
            }
            stopSelf()
            return START_NOT_STICKY
        }

        val notification = LockScreenRadarNotification.buildNotification(this)
        if (notification != null) {
            try {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.UPSIDE_DOWN_CAKE) {
                    try {
                        startForeground(
                            LockScreenRadarNotification.NOTIFICATION_ID,
                            notification,
                            ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_SPECIAL_USE
                        )
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        startForeground(
                            LockScreenRadarNotification.NOTIFICATION_ID,
                            notification,
                            ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_DATA_SYNC
                        )
                    }
                } else if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                    startForeground(
                        LockScreenRadarNotification.NOTIFICATION_ID,
                        notification,
                        ServiceInfo.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_TYPE_DATA_SYNC
                    )
                } else {
                    startForeground(LockScreenRadarNotification.NOTIFICATION_ID, notification)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        } else {
            stopSelf()
        }

        return START_STICKY
    }

    override fun onTimeout(startId: Int) {
        try {
            stopSelf(startId)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    override fun onTimeout(startId: Int, fgsType: Int) {
        try {
            stopSelf(startId)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            stopSelf()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val ACTION_START = "com.example.frontend.START_RADAR_FOREGROUND"
        const val ACTION_STOP = "com.example.frontend.STOP_RADAR_FOREGROUND"

        fun start(context: Context) {
            val intent = Intent(context, RadarForegroundService::class.java).apply {
                action = ACTION_START
            }
            try {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                    context.startForegroundService(intent)
                } else {
                    context.startService(intent)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }

        fun stop(context: Context) {
            val intent = Intent(context, RadarForegroundService::class.java).apply {
                action = ACTION_STOP
            }
            try {
                context.startService(intent)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: frontend/lib/data/kyiv_gazetteer.dart
------------------------------------------------------------
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

String? _cachedAppFilesDir;

Future<String?> _getAppFilesDirPath() async {
  if (_cachedAppFilesDir != null) return _cachedAppFilesDir;
  if (!kIsWeb && Platform.isAndroid) {
    try {
      const platform = MethodChannel('ua.kyiv.alert.monitor/widget');
      final String? path = await platform.invokeMethod<String>('getFilesDir');
      if (path != null && path.isNotEmpty) {
        _cachedAppFilesDir = path;
        return path;
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  } else if (!kIsWeb && Platform.isWindows) {
    final envAppdata = Platform.environment['APPDATA'];
    if (envAppdata != null) {
      final dir = Directory('$envAppdata/KyivAlert');
      if (!dir.existsSync()) dir.createSync(recursive: true);
      _cachedAppFilesDir = dir.path;
      return dir.path;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

Future<File?> _getAppStorageFile(String fileName) async {
  try {
    final normalized = fileName.trim();
    if (normalized.isEmpty ||
        normalized.contains('..') ||
        normalized.contains('\\') ||
        normalized.contains(':') ||
        normalized.startsWith('/')) {
      return null;
    }
    final dirPath = await _getAppFilesDirPath();
    if (dirPath != null) {
      return File('$dirPath/$normalized');
    }
    return File(normalized);
  } catch (_) {
    return null;
  }
}


Future<List<Map<String, dynamic>>> searchStreetAddress(String query) async {
  final trimmed = query.trim();
  if (trimmed.isEmpty || trimmed.length > 100) return [];
  try {
    final uri = Uri.parse(
      'https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=json&q=${Uri.encodeComponent('$trimmed, Київська область')}&limit=5&accept-language=uk',
    );
    final response = await http.get(
      uri,
      headers: {'User-Agent': 'ua.kyiv.alert.monitor'},
    ).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 4));
    if (response.statusCode == 200 && response.bodyBytes.length <= 262144) {
      final List data = json.decode(utf8.decode(response.bodyBytes));
      return data.map((item) => {
        'display_name': (item['display_name'] ?? '').toString(),
        'lat': double.tryParse(item['lat']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0.0,
        'lon': double.tryParse(item['lon']?.toString() ?? '') ?? 0.0,
      }).where((m) => (m['lat'] as double) != 0.0).toList();
    }
  } catch (_) {}
  return [];
}


const Set<String> kExternalBorderKeys = {
  'житомирщин', 'житомирськ', 'житомирської', 'житомир', 'малин', 'коростень', 'радомишл', 'овруч',

Map<String, (double, double, String)> kKyivLocations = Map.of(kKyivRegionLocations);
Map<String, (double, double, String)> _learnedLocations = {};
final Set<String> _pendingOrFailedGeocodes = {};
VoidCallback? onNewLocationLearned;

String stemLocationCandidate(String word) {
  var lower = word.toLowerCase();
  const vowels = {'а', 'я', 'у', 'ю', 'і', 'и', 'о', 'е'};
  if (lower.length >= 6 && vowels.contains(lower[lower.length - 1])) {
    lower = lower.substring(0, lower.length - 1);
  }
  return lower;
}

const _kLocationStopWords = <String>{
  'київщина', 'київська', 'київ', 'увага', 'тривога', 'шахед', 'шахеди', 'шахеда',
  'ракета', 'ракети', 'балістика', 'північ', 'південь', 'захід', 'схід',
  'дніпро', 'україна', 'область', 'області', 'район', 'району', 'сектор',
  'напрямок', 'напрямку', 'вектор', 'курс', 'пуски', 'виходи', 'збиття',
  'відбій', 'чисто', 'повітряна', 'небезпека', 'група', 'групи', 'ціль', 'цілі',
  'борщ', 'борщу', 'борщем', 'азс', 'заправка', 'заправки', 'заправку', 'кло'
};

Future<void> _loadLearnedLocations() async {
  try {
    final file = await _getAppStorageFile('learned_locations.json');
    if (file != null && await file.exists()) {
      if (await file.length() > 524288) return;
      final content = await file.readAsString();
      final Map<String, dynamic> data = json.decode(content);
      int count = 0;
      for (final entry in data.entries) {
        if (count >= 300) break;
        final keyStr = entry.key.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (keyStr.length < 3 || keyStr.length > 35) continue;
        if (keyStr.startsWith('лук\'ян') || keyStr.startsWith('лукʼян') || keyStr.startsWith('лук’ян') || keyStr.startsWith('лукян')) {
          continue; // Лук'янівка — виключно метро Лук'янівка (Київ)!
        }
        final val = entry.value;
        if (val is Map) {
          final lat = (val['lat'] as num?)?.toDouble();
          final lon = (val['lon'] as num?)?.toDouble();
          final rawName = (val['name'] ?? keyStr).toString();
          final cleanName = rawName.replaceAll(RegExp(r'<[^>]*>'), '').replaceAll(RegExp(r'[\r\n\t]'), ' ').trim();
          final safeName = cleanName.length > 40 ? cleanName.substring(0, 40) : cleanName;
          if (lat != null && lon != null) {
            if (lat >= 49.1 && lat <= 51.6 && lon >= 29.2 && lon <= 32.2) {
              _learnedLocations[keyStr] = (lat, lon, safeName);
              if (!kKyivRegionLocations.containsKey(keyStr)) {
                kKyivLocations[keyStr] = (lat, lon, safeName);
              }
              count++;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}

Future<void> _saveLearnedLocations() async {
  try {
    final file = await _getAppStorageFile('learned_locations.json');
    if (file != null) {
      final Map<String, dynamic> out = {};
      for (final e in _learnedLocations.entries) {
        out[e.key] = {
          'lat': e.value.$1,
          'lon': e.value.$2,
          'name': e.value.$3,
        };
      }
      await file.writeAsString(json.encode(out));
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}

Future<void> _autoGeocodeLocation(String rawWord, String stem) async {
  try {
    if (stem.length < 3 || stem.length > 35 || _learnedLocations.length >= 300) return;
    final uri = Uri.parse(
      'https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=json&q=${Uri.encodeComponent("$rawWord, Київська область")}&viewbox=29.2,51.6,32.2,49.1&bounded=1&limit=1&accept-language=uk'
    );
    final response = await http.get(uri, headers: {
      'User-Agent': 'ua.kyiv.alert.monitor/1.0 (contact: admin@kyivalert.ua)',
    }).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 4));

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      final list = json.decode(response.body);
      if (list is List && list.isNotEmpty) {
        final item = list[0] as Map<String, dynamic>;
        final lat = double.tryParse(item['lat']?.toString() ?? '');
        final lon = double.tryParse(item['lon']?.toString() ?? '');
        if (lat != null && lon != null) {
          if (lat >= 49.1 && lat <= 51.6 && lon >= 29.2 && lon <= 32.2 && _learnedLocations.length < 300) {
            final rawDisplay = item['display_name']?.toString().split(',')[0].trim() ?? rawWord;
            final cleanName = rawDisplay.replaceAll(RegExp(r'<[^>]*>'), '').replaceAll(RegExp(r'[\r\n\t]'), ' ').trim();
            final safeName = cleanName.length > 40 ? cleanName.substring(0, 40) : cleanName;
            _learnedLocations[stem] = (lat, lon, safeName);
            if (!kKyivRegionLocations.containsKey(stem)) {
              kKyivLocations[stem] = (lat, lon, safeName);
            }
            await _saveLearnedLocations();
            onNewLocationLearned?.call();
          }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (_) {}
}

void _checkAndAutoGeocodeLocations(String text) {
  if (_learnedLocations.length >= 300) return;
  final candidateRegex = RegExp(
    r'(?:на|через|біля|поблизу|район[іу]?|повз|від|з|із|в бік|у бік|курсом на|вектор на)\s+([А-ЯІЇЄҐ][а-яіїєґ\x27ʼ\-]{3,35})'
  );
  for (final m in candidateRegex.allMatches(text)) {
    final rawWord = m.group(1);
    if (rawWord == null) continue;
    final rawLower = rawWord.toLowerCase();
    if (_kLocationStopWords.contains(rawLower)) continue;
    final stem = stemLocationCandidate(rawWord);
    if (stem.length < 3 || stem.length > 35) continue;
    if (!kKyivLocations.containsKey(stem) && !_pendingOrFailedGeocodes.contains(stem)) {
      _pendingOrFailedGeocodes.add(stem);
      _autoGeocodeLocation(rawWord, stem);
      break; // Максимум 1 нове геокодування за один прохід для захисту від бану Nominatim (1 запит/сек)
    }
  }
}
List<String> dynamicExtraChannels = [];

List<String> dynamicClearKeywords = [];

Map<String, (double, double, String)> get learnedLocations => _learnedLocations;

FILE: frontend/lib/desktop_map_wrapper.dart
------------------------------------------------------------
import 'package:flutter/gestures.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';

class DesktopMapWrapper extends StatefulWidget {
  final MapController mapController;
  final Widget child;

  const DesktopMapWrapper({super.key, required this.mapController, required this.child});

  @override
  State<DesktopMapWrapper> createState() => _DesktopMapWrapperState();
}

class _DesktopMapWrapperState extends State<DesktopMapWrapper> {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Listener(
      onPointerSignal: (PointerSignalEvent event) {
        if (event is PointerScrollEvent) {
          final camera = widget.mapController.camera;
          
          // Detect mouse wheel vs trackpad scroll. 
          // Mouse wheel on Windows usually emits a delta of exactly 120.0 or 100.0, or at least > 40.
          // Trackpad scrolls have small fractional values and frequent events.
          bool isMouseWheel = event.kind == PointerDeviceKind.mouse && (event.scrollDelta.dy.abs() >= 40.0 || event.scrollDelta.dx == 0 && event.scrollDelta.dy.abs() % 10 == 0);
          
          if (isMouseWheel) {
            // ZOOOOOM
            final zoomDelta = event.scrollDelta.dy > 0 ? -0.5 : 0.5;
            final newZoom = (camera.zoom + zoomDelta).clamp(5.0, 18.0);
            widget.mapController.move(camera.center, newZoom);
          } else {
            // PAAAN
            final currentCenter = camera.latLngToScreenOffset(camera.center);
            final newCenterPixels = Offset(currentCenter.dx + event.scrollDelta.dx, currentCenter.dy + event.scrollDelta.dy);
            final newCenterLatLng = camera.screenOffsetToLatLng(newCenterPixels);
            widget.mapController.move(newCenterLatLng, camera.zoom);
          }
        }
      },
      onPointerPanZoomUpdate: (PointerPanZoomUpdateEvent event) {
        final camera = widget.mapController.camera;
        if (event.scale != 1.0) {
          // ZOOOOOM
          // event.scale is relative to 1.0. For example, 1.1 means zoom in by 10%.
          // We map it to map zoom levels:
          final zoomDelta = (event.scale - 1.0) * 2.0; 
          final newZoom = (camera.zoom + zoomDelta).clamp(5.0, 18.0);
          widget.mapController.move(camera.center, newZoom);
        } else if (event.panDelta.dx != 0 || event.panDelta.dy != 0) {
          // PAAAN
          final currentCenter = camera.latLngToScreenOffset(camera.center);
          final newCenterPixels = Offset(currentCenter.dx - event.panDelta.dx, currentCenter.dy - event.panDelta.dy);
          final newCenterLatLng = camera.screenOffsetToLatLng(newCenterPixels);
          widget.mapController.move(newCenterLatLng, camera.zoom);
        }
      },
      child: widget.child,
    );
  }
}
