import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;

/// Сервіс безпечного завантаження та встановлення оновлень APK
class UpdateService {
  static const allowedHosts = {
    'github.com',
    'objects.githubusercontent.com',
    'raw.githubusercontent.com'
  };

  /// Перевірка безпеки URL: виключно HTTPS та довірені сервери GitHub
  static bool isTrustedHttps(String apkUrl) {
    final uri = Uri.tryParse(apkUrl);
    return uri != null &&
        uri.scheme == 'https' &&
        allowedHosts.any((h) => uri.host == h || uri.host.endsWith('.$h'));
  }

  /// Завантаження та виклик нативного інсталятора з контролем DoS (150 МБ) та сигнатури ZIP
  static Future<void> downloadAndInstallApk({
    required String apkUrl,
    required void Function(double progress) onProgress,
    required void Function(String error) onError,
    required void Function(String filePath) onSavedNonAndroid,
    required void Function() onPermissionRequired,
    required void Function(bool isDownloading) onDownloadingChanged,
  }) async {
    if (!isTrustedHttps(apkUrl)) {
      onError('Помилка безпеки: завантаження APK дозволено виключно через HTTPS з офіційних серверів GitHub');
      return;
    }

    onDownloadingChanged(true);
    onProgress(0.0);

    try {
      final uri = Uri.parse(apkUrl);
      String cacheDir = '';
      if (!kIsWeb && Platform.isAndroid) {
        try {
          const channel = MethodChannel('ua.kyiv.alert.monitor/widget');
          cacheDir = await channel.invokeMethod<String>('getCacheDir') ?? '';
        } catch (_) {}
      }

      File apkFile;
      if (cacheDir.isNotEmpty) {
        apkFile = File('$cacheDir/update.apk');
      } else {
        final envTemp = Platform.environment['TEMP'] ?? Platform.environment['TMP'];
        apkFile = File(envTemp != null ? '$envTemp/update.apk' : 'update.apk');
      }

      // Видаляємо попередній файл оновлення перед завантаженням
      if (await apkFile.exists()) {
        try {
          await apkFile.delete();
        } catch (_) {}
      }

      const int maxApkBytes = 157286400; // 150 МБ ліміт проти DoS
      final client = http.Client();
      try {
        final request = http.Request('GET', uri);
        final response = await client.send(request);

        if (response.statusCode != 200) {
          throw Exception('Сервер повернув код ${response.statusCode}');
        }

        final totalBytes = response.contentLength ?? 0;
        if (totalBytes > maxApkBytes) {
          throw Exception('Розмір оновлення ($totalBytes байт) перевищує ліміт безпеки (150 МБ)');
        }

        int receivedBytes = 0;
        final sink = apkFile.openWrite();
        try {
          await for (final chunk in response.stream.timeout(const Duration(seconds: 45))) {
            receivedBytes += chunk.length;
            if (receivedBytes > maxApkBytes) {
              throw Exception('Перевищено максимальний ліміт розміру файлу (150 МБ)');
            }
            sink.add(chunk);
            if (totalBytes > 0) {
              onProgress((receivedBytes / totalBytes).clamp(0.0, 1.0));
            }
          }
          await sink.flush();
        } finally {
          await sink.close();
        }
      } finally {
        client.close();
      }

      // Перевірка сигнатури APK (ZIP Magic Header: 0x50, 0x4B, 0x03, 0x04)
      if (await apkFile.exists()) {
        final headerBytes = await apkFile.openRead(0, 4).first;
        final bool isZipMagic = headerBytes.length >= 4 &&
            headerBytes[0] == 0x50 &&
            headerBytes[1] == 0x4B &&
            headerBytes[2] == 0x03 &&
            headerBytes[3] == 0x04;
        if (!isZipMagic) {
          try {
            await apkFile.delete();
          } catch (_) {}
          throw Exception('Помилка безпеки: завантажений файл не є валідним APK-пакетом (невірна сигнатура)');
        }
      } else {
        throw Exception('Файл оновлення не знайдено після завантаження');
      }

      onDownloadingChanged(false);

      if (!kIsWeb && Platform.isAndroid) {
        const channel = MethodChannel('ua.kyiv.alert.monitor/widget');
        try {
          await channel.invokeMethod('installApk', {'filePath': apkFile.path});
        } on PlatformException catch (pe) {
          if (pe.code == 'PERMISSION_REQUIRED') {
            onPermissionRequired();
            return;
          }
          rethrow;
        }
      } else {
        onSavedNonAndroid(apkFile.path);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      onDownloadingChanged(false);
      onError('Помилка оновлення: $e');
    }
  }
}

FILE: frontend/lib/services/voice_alert_service.dart
------------------------------------------------------------
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_tts/flutter_tts.dart';
import 'package:frontend/data/kyiv_gazetteer.dart';

/// Сервіс голосового сповіщення (TTS) для "Нічного вартового" та критичних загроз
class VoiceAlertService {
  static final VoiceAlertService instance = VoiceAlertService._internal();
  VoiceAlertService._internal();

  FlutterTts? _flutterTts;
  bool _isInitialized = false;
  bool _isEnabled = true;
  DateTime? _mutedUntil;

  // Захист від повторів (cooldown 120 сек) для кожної цілі
  final Map<String, DateTime> _threatCooldowns = {};

  bool get isEnabled => _isEnabled;
  bool get isMuted => _mutedUntil != null && DateTime.now().isBefore(_mutedUntil!);

  /// Увімкнення/вимкнення голосу з персистентним збереженням
  Future<void> setEnabled(bool enabled) async {
    _isEnabled = enabled;
    if (!enabled) {
      await stop();
    }
    await _saveSettings();
  }

  /// Тимчасова тиша (наприклад, 15 хвилин)
  void muteFor(Duration duration) {
    _mutedUntil = DateTime.now().add(duration);
    stop();
  }

  void unmute() {
    _mutedUntil = null;
  }

  /// Ініціалізація рушія TTS
  Future<void> init() async {
    if (_isInitialized || kIsWeb || Platform.environment.containsKey('FLUTTER_TEST')) return;
    try {
      await _loadSettings();
      _flutterTts = FlutterTts();

      // Налаштування української мови та спокійного темпу
      await _flutterTts!.setLanguage('uk-UA');
      await _flutterTts!.setSpeechRate(0.52);
      await _flutterTts!.setPitch(1.0);

      if (!kIsWeb && Platform.isAndroid) {
        await _flutterTts!.setVolume(1.0);
      }

      _isInitialized = true;
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('VoiceAlertService init error: $e');
    }
  }

  /// Зупинити поточне мовлення
  Future<void> stop() async {
    try {
      await _flutterTts?.stop();
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  /// Озвучення довільного тексту українською
  Future<void> speak(String text) async {
    if (!_isEnabled || isMuted) return;
    try {
      if (!_isInitialized) {
        await init();
      }
      if (_flutterTts != null) {
        await _flutterTts!.stop();
        await _flutterTts!.speak(text);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('VoiceAlertService speak error: $e');
    }
  }

  /// Озвучення загрози балістики для Києва та області
  Future<void> announceBallistics() async {
    const key = 'ballistics_alert';
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final lastTime = _threatCooldowns[key];
    if (lastTime != null && now.difference(lastTime).inSeconds < 120) {
      return; // 2 хвилини cooldown
    }
    _threatCooldowns[key] = now;

    await speak('Увага! Загроза балістики для Києва та області. Перейдіть в укриття!');
  }

  /// Озвучення небезпеки біля зони безпеки
  Future<void> announceThreatNearZone({
    required String threatKey,
    required String threatType,
    required String zoneName,
    required double distanceKm,
    required double etaMinutes,
  }) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final lastTime = _threatCooldowns[threatKey];
    if (lastTime != null && now.difference(lastTime).inSeconds < 120) {
      return; // 2 хвилини cooldown для цієї цілі
    }
    _threatCooldowns[threatKey] = now;

    final friendlyType = toFriendlyVoiceThreatType(threatType);
    final distStr = distanceKm < 10 ? distanceKm.toStringAsFixed(1) : distanceKm.round().toString();
    final etaStr = etaMinutes.ceil().clamp(1, 120).toString();

    final text = 'Увага! $friendlyType поблизу зони $zoneName. Відстань $distStr кілометрів, час підльоту близько $etaStr хвилин.';
    await speak(text);
  }

  /// Перетворення системного типу загрози на природний український голос
  static String toFriendlyVoiceThreatType(String type) {
    switch (type) {
      case 'shahed_jet':
        return 'Реактивний БпЛА';
      case 'shahed':
        return 'Ударний БпЛА';
      case 'missile_ballistic':
        return 'Балістична ціль';
      case 'missile_cruise':
        return 'Крилата ракета';
      case 'missile_hypersonic':
        return 'Гіперзвукова ракета';
      case 'kab':
        return 'Керована авіабомба';
      case 'uav_recon':
        return 'Розвідувальний дрон';
      case 'uav_guided':
        return 'Керований дрон';
      default:
        return 'Повітряна ціль';
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadSettings() async {
    try {
      final file = await getAppStorageFile('voice_settings.json');
      if (file != null && await file.exists()) {
        final content = await file.readAsString();
        final map = json.decode(content) as Map<String, dynamic>;
        if (map['enabled'] is bool) {
          _isEnabled = map['enabled'] as bool;
        }
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> _saveSettings() async {
    try {
      final file = await getAppStorageFile('voice_settings.json');
      if (file != null) {
        await file.writeAsString(json.encode({'enabled': _isEnabled}));
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }
}

FILE: frontend/lib/ui/sheets/morning_digest_sheet.dart
------------------------------------------------------------
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';
import 'package:frontend/models/alert_models.dart';
import 'package:frontend/services/threat_parser.dart';

class _ExpandableNewsItem extends StatefulWidget {
  final String text;
  final Color bulletColor;
  final TextStyle style;

  const _ExpandableNewsItem({
    required this.text,
    required this.bulletColor,
    required this.style,
  });

  @override
  State<_ExpandableNewsItem> createState() => _ExpandableNewsItemState();
}

class _ExpandableNewsItemState extends State<_ExpandableNewsItem> {
  bool _isExpanded = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: () {
          setState(() {
            _isExpanded = !_isExpanded;
          });
        },
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
        child: Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text('• ', style: TextStyle(color: widget.bulletColor, fontSize: 14)),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                widget.text,
                maxLines: _isExpanded ? null : 4,
                overflow: _isExpanded ? TextOverflow.visible : TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: widget.style,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


  Widget _buildDigestStatCard(String title, String count, IconData icon, Color color) {
    return Expanded(
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8, horizontal: 4),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.1),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
          border: Border.all(color: color.withValues(alpha: 0.25)),
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Icon(icon, color: color, size: 16),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text(
              count,
              style: TextStyle(color: color, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 2),
            Text(
              title,
              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade400, fontSize: 10),
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }



  void showMorningDigestSheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  required List<dynamic> messages,
  required List<WatchedLocation> watchedLocations,
  bool isNightWatchMode = false,
}) {
    showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF16161E),
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
      ),
      builder: (sheetContext) {
        bool isLoadingNews = true;
        List<String> energyNews = [];
        List<String> transportNews = [];
        bool hasInitiatedFetch = false;

        Future<List<String>> fetchTelegramPreview(String channel, int limit) async {
          try {
            final response = await http.get(
              Uri.parse('https://t.me/s/$channel'),
              headers: {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)'},
            ).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 7));
            if (response.statusCode == 200) {
              final html = response.body;
              final blocks = html.split('tgme_widget_message_wrap');
              final list = <String>[];
              final startIdx = blocks.length > limit * 3 ? blocks.length - limit * 3 : 1;
              for (int i = startIdx; i < blocks.length; i++) {
                final block = blocks[i];
                final mainTextMatches = RegExp(r'tgme_widget_message_text(?![^>]*reply)[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)</div>').allMatches(block);
                if (mainTextMatches.isNotEmpty) {
                  final raw = mainTextMatches.first.group(1);
                  if (raw != null) {
                    final clean = cleanHtmlString(raw, maxLength: 3500);
                    if (clean.isNotEmpty) {
                      list.add(clean);
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
              if (list.length > limit) {
                return list.sublist(list.length - limit);
              }
              return list;
            }
          } catch (_) {}
          return [];
        }

        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (ctx, setSheetState) {
            // messages passed as argument
            // watchedLocations passed as argument

            // Автономне / бекенд завантаження новин при відкритті
            if (!hasInitiatedFetch) {
              hasInitiatedFetch = true;
              Future.microtask(() async {
                try {
                  // 1. Спроба завантажити з бекенду (тільки у Debug-режимі)
                  if (kDebugMode) {
                    try {
                      final res = await http.get(Uri.parse('http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/digest')).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 3));
                      if (res.statusCode == 200) {
                        final data = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(res.bodyBytes));
                        if (data is Map && data['energy_news'] is List && data['transport_news'] is List) {
                          setSheetState(() {
                            energyNews = (data['energy_news'] as List).map((e) => cleanHtmlString(e.toString(), maxLength: 3500)).where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).toList();
                            transportNews = (data['transport_news'] as List).map((e) => cleanHtmlString(e.toString(), maxLength: 3500)).where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).toList();
                            isLoadingNews = false;
                          });
                          return;
                        }
                      }
                    } catch (_) {}
                  }

                  // 2. Автономний режим: прямий скрапінг t.me/s/dtek_ua та t.me/s/kyivpasstrans
                  final dtek = await fetchTelegramPreview('dtek_ua', 6);
                  final kova = await fetchTelegramPreview('Kyivoda', 5);
                  var kpt = await fetchTelegramPreview('kyivpasstrans', 8);
                  if (kpt.isEmpty) {
                    kpt = await fetchTelegramPreview('kyivpastrans', 8);
                  }

                  final filteredEnergy = <String>[];
                  for (final m in (dtek + kova).reversed) {
                    final mLower = m.toLowerCase();
                    if (mLower.contains('київ') || mLower.contains('графік') || mLower.contains('відключен') || mLower.contains('світл') || mLower.contains('енерг') || mLower.contains('дтек') || mLower.contains('аварійн')) {
                      if (!filteredEnergy.contains(m)) {
                        filteredEnergy.add(m);
                      }
                    }
                  }

                  final filteredTransport = <String>[];
                  for (final m in kpt.reversed) {
                    final mLower = m.toLowerCase();
                    if (mLower.contains('святошин') || mLower.contains('борщагів') || mLower.contains('трамвай') || mLower.contains('курбаса') || mLower.contains('гната юри') || mLower.contains('кільцев')) {
                      filteredTransport.insert(0, '⭐ [Святошин/Борщагівка] $m');
                    } else if (mLower.contains('рух') || mLower.contains('затримк') || mLower.contains('змін') || mLower.contains('маршрут') || mLower.contains('поновлен') || mLower.contains('метро')) {
                      filteredTransport.add(m);
                    }
                  }

                  setSheetState(() {
                    energyNews = filteredEnergy.take(4).map((e) => cleanHtmlString(e, maxLength: 3500)).where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).toList();
                    transportNews = filteredTransport.take(4).map((e) => cleanHtmlString(e, maxLength: 3500)).where((s) => s.isNotEmpty).toList();
                    isLoadingNews = false;
                  });
                } catch (_) {
                  setSheetState(() {
                    isLoadingNews = false;
                  });
                }
              });
            }

            // Розрахунок нічної статистики (за останні 10 годин)
            final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
            final cutoff = now.subtract(const Duration(hours: 10));

            int totalThreats = 0;
            int uavCount = 0;
            int missileCount = 0;
            int ppoMentions = 0;
            DateTime? lastClearDt;
            DateTime? activeBallisticUntil;

            const ppoKeywords = ['ппо', 'працює ппо', 'робота ппо', 'роботі ппо', 'збиття', 'збито', 'мінус', 'ліквідовано'];
            const clearKeywords = ['відбій', 'чисто', 'тихо', 'дорозвідка', 'локаційно втрачено', 'немає цілей'];
            const missileKeywords = [
              'ракет', 'крилаті', 'баліст', 'іскандер', 'кинджал', 'циркон', 'калібр',
              'х-101', 'х-22', 'х-32', 'х-59', 'х-69', 'онікс', 'с-300', 'с-400',
              'швидкісна', 'швидкісні', 'виходи', 'багато цілей', 'цілей йде'
            ];
            const negativeMissileWords = ['не підтверд', 'не фіксу', 'хибн', 'відбій', 'чисто'];

            final Set<String> threatenedZoneNames = {};
            final activeZones = watchedLocations.where((l) => l.enabled).toList();

            // Фільтруємо повідомлення за останні 10 годин і сортуємо хронологічно
            final recentMessages = messages.where((msg) {
              if (msg['date'] == null) return false;
              try {
                final t = DateTime.parse(msg['date'].toString()).toUtc();
                return !t.isBefore(cutoff);
              } catch (_) {
                return false;
              }
            }).toList();

            recentMessages.sort((a, b) {
              final da = DateTime.tryParse(a['date']?.toString() ?? '')?.toUtc() ?? DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
              final db = DateTime.tryParse(b['date']?.toString() ?? '')?.toUtc() ?? DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
              return da.compareTo(db);
            });

            for (final msg in recentMessages) {
              DateTime? msgTime;
              try {
                msgTime = DateTime.parse(msg['date'].toString()).toUtc();
              } catch (_) {}

              final text = (msg['text'] ?? '').toString();
              final tLower = text.toLowerCase();
              final bool hasNegative = negativeMissileWords.any((nw) => tLower.contains(nw));

              // Перевірка на тригери ракет / балістики: ТІЛЬКИ явні ракетні слова, '!!!!' або одне-два слова капсом без локацій
              final bool isExplicitBallisticTrigger = !hasNegative && (
                missileKeywords.any((mk) => tLower.contains(mk)) ||
                RegExp(r'^(?:УВАГА|ЦІЛЬ|ПУСК|ВИХОДИ|ШВИДКІСНА|БАЛІСТИКА)[!\s⚠️]*$').hasMatch(text.trim()) ||
                text.contains('!!!!')
              );

              if (isExplicitBallisticTrigger && msgTime != null) {
                final windowEnd = msgTime.add(const Duration(minutes: 4));
                if (activeBallisticUntil == null || windowEnd.isAfter(activeBallisticUntil)) {
                  activeBallisticUntil = windowEnd;
                }
              }

              final bool inBallisticWindow = msgTime != null &&
                  activeBallisticUntil != null &&
                  (msgTime.isBefore(activeBallisticUntil) || msgTime.isAtSameMomentAs(activeBallisticUntil));

              final threats = (msg['threats'] as List?) ?? [];
              final bool textHasMissile = !hasNegative && (
                missileKeywords.any((mk) => tLower.contains(mk)) ||
                inBallisticWindow
              );

              for (final t in threats) {
                totalThreats++;
                final tType = (t['type'] ?? 'shahed').toString().toLowerCase();
                final tRaw = (t['rawText'] ?? '').toString().toLowerCase();

                final bool isMissile = inBallisticWindow ||
                    tType.contains('missile') ||
                    tType.contains('ballistic') ||
                    tType.contains('hypersonic') ||
                    tType.contains('cruise') ||
                    tType.contains('supersonic') ||
                    tType == 'kab' ||
                    textHasMissile ||
                    (!negativeMissileWords.any((nw) => tRaw.contains(nw)) &&
                        missileKeywords.any((mk) => tRaw.contains(mk)));

                if (isMissile) {
                  missileCount++;
                } else {
                  uavCount++;
                }

                final path = (t['path'] as List?) ?? [];
                for (final loc in activeZones) {
                  final double effectiveRadius = loc.radiusMeters + (isNightWatchMode ? 3500.0 : 0.0);
                  for (final p in path) {
                    double? lat, lon;
                    if (p is LatLng) {
                      lat = p.latitude;
                      lon = p.longitude;
                    } else if (p is Map && p['lat'] != null && p['lon'] != null) {
                      lat = (p['lat'] as num).toDouble();
                      lon = (p['lon'] as num).toDouble();
                    }
                    if (lat != null && lon != null) {
                      final dist = const Distance().as(LengthUnit.Meter, loc.point, LatLng(lat, lon));
                      if (dist <= effectiveRadius) {
                        threatenedZoneNames.add(loc.name);
                        break;
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }
              }

              // Якщо в пості не було окремих parsed threats, але є ракетні слова або діє балістичне вікно:
              if (threats.isEmpty && (textHasMissile || isExplicitBallisticTrigger || inBallisticWindow)) {
                totalThreats++;
                missileCount++;
              }

              if (ppoKeywords.any((pk) => tLower.contains(pk))) {
                ppoMentions++;
              }

              if (msg['type'] == 'clear' || clearKeywords.any((ck) => tLower.contains(ck))) {
                if (msgTime != null) {
                  if (lastClearDt == null || msgTime.isAfter(lastClearDt)) {
                    lastClearDt = msgTime;
                  }
                }
              }
            }

            final String lastClearStr = lastClearDt != null
                ? '${lastClearDt.toLocal().hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${lastClearDt.toLocal().minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}'
                : 'Не зафіксовано';

            final String zonesStatusStr = threatenedZoneNames.isEmpty
                ? '🟢 Жодна ціль не наближалась до ваших зон безпеки'
                : '⚠️ Зафіксовано прольоти поблизу: ${threatenedZoneNames.join(', ')}';

            final String currentTimeStr = '${DateTime.now().hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${DateTime.now().minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

            return Container(
              constraints: BoxConstraints(
                maxHeight: MediaQuery.of(sheetContext).size.height * 0.88,
              ),
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(
                bottom: MediaQuery.of(sheetContext).viewInsets.bottom + 16,
                left: 16,
                right: 16,
                top: 12,
              ),
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Column(

                            ),

                                text: cleanItem,
                                bulletColor: Colors.amberAccent,
                                style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade300, fontSize: 12, height: 1.3),
                              );

                              final cleanItem = cleanHtmlString(item, maxLength: 3500);
                              final bool isPriority = cleanItem.contains('[Святошин/Борщагівка]');
                              return _ExpandableNewsItem(
                                text: cleanItem,
                                bulletColor: isPriority ? Colors.amberAccent : Colors.cyanAccent,
                                style: TextStyle(

                                  fontWeight: isPriority ? FontWeight.w500 : FontWeight.normal,

                              '• Громадський транспорт та швидкісний трамвай курсують у штатному режимі.',
                              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade400, fontSize: 12),
                            ),
                          ],

                      ),

                    Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(

                            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh, size: 18),
                            label: const Text('Оновити'),

                              foregroundColor: Colors.white,

                            ),
                            onPressed: () async {
                              final fullReport = StringBuffer();
                              fullReport.writeln('☕ РАНКОВИЙ БРИФІНГ: КИЇВ ТА ОБЛАСТЬ ($currentTimeStr)');
                              fullReport.writeln('\n🛡️ ПІДСУМКИ НОЧІ (за 10 год):');
                              fullReport.writeln('• Зафіксовано загроз: $totalThreats');
                              fullReport.writeln('• Шахеди: $uavCount');
                              fullReport.writeln('• Ракети/Балістика: $missileCount');
                              fullReport.writeln('• Робота ППО: $ppoMentions');
                              fullReport.writeln('• Останній відбій: $lastClearStr');
                              fullReport.writeln('• Зони безпеки: $zonesStatusStr');

                              fullReport.writeln('\n⚡ СТАН ЕНЕРГЕТИКИ (ДТЕК):');
                              if (energyNews.isNotEmpty) {
                                for (final en in energyNews.take(2)) {
                                  fullReport.writeln('• $en');
                                }
                              } else {
                                fullReport.writeln('• Наразі повідомлень про екстрені відключення немає.');
                              }

                              fullReport.writeln('\n🚋 ТРАНСПОРТ ТА ІНФРАСТРУКТУРА:');
                              if (transportNews.isNotEmpty) {
                                for (final tn in transportNews.take(3)) {
                                  fullReport.writeln('• $tn');
                                }
                              } else {
                                fullReport.writeln('• Рух громадського транспорту у штатному режимі.');
                              }

                              fullReport.writeln('\nБезпечного та мирного дня! 🇺🇦');

                              await Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: fullReport.toString()));
                              if (context.mounted) {
                                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                  const SnackBar(
                                    content: Text(
                                      '📋 Ранковий звіт успішно скопійовано!',
                                      style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                                    ),
                                    backgroundColor: Colors.purple,
                                    duration: Duration(seconds: 2),
                                  ),
                                );
                              }
                            },
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );
  }

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_map/flutter_map.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:latlong2/latlong.dart';
import 'package:frontend/data/kyiv_gazetteer.dart';
import 'package:frontend/models/alert_models.dart';
import 'package:frontend/services/voice_alert_service.dart';


Future<bool> _isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations() async {
  if (kIsWeb || !Platform.isAndroid) return true;
  try {
    const channel = MethodChannel('ua.kyiv.alert.monitor/widget');
    final bool? res = await channel.invokeMethod<bool>('isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations');
    return res ?? false;
  } catch (_) {
    return false;
  }
}

Future<void> _requestIgnoreBatteryOptimizations() async {
  if (kIsWeb || !Platform.isAndroid) return;
  try {
    const channel = MethodChannel('ua.kyiv.alert.monitor/widget');
    await channel.invokeMethod('requestIgnoreBatteryOptimizations');
  } catch (_) {}
}


  void showWatchedLocationsSheet({
  required BuildContext context,
  required WidgetRef ref,
  required MapController mapController,
  required dynamic watchedLocationsProvider,
}) async {
    final searchCtrl = TextEditingController();
    final nameCtrl = TextEditingController();
    double radiusKm = 4.0;
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> searchResults = [];
    bool isSearching = false;
    bool isAddingNew = false;
    Map<String, dynamic>? selectedResult;

    await showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF1E1E1E),
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (ctx) {
        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (sheetContext, setSheetState) {
            final watchedList = ref.watch(watchedLocationsProvider);

            return Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(
                bottom: MediaQuery.of(sheetContext).viewInsets.bottom + 16,
                left: 16,
                right: 16,
                top: 12,
              ),
              child: SingleChildScrollView(
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Center(
                      child: Container(
                        width: 40,
                        height: 4,
                        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Row(
                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                      children: [
                        const Row(
                          children: [
                            Icon(Icons.shield_outlined, color: Colors.purpleAccent, size: 22),
                            SizedBox(width: 8),
                            Text(
                              'Мої зони безпеки',
                              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.white),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            IconButton(
                              tooltip: VoiceAlertService.instance.isEnabled
                                  ? 'Голосовий диктор (Увімкнено)'
                                  : 'Голосовий диктор (Вимкнено)',
                              icon: Icon(
                                VoiceAlertService.instance.isEnabled
                                    ? Icons.record_voice_over
                                    : Icons.voice_over_off,
                                color: VoiceAlertService.instance.isEnabled
                                    ? Colors.cyanAccent
                                    : Colors.grey,
                                size: 22,
                              ),
                              onPressed: () async {
                                await VoiceAlertService.instance.setEnabled(!VoiceAlertService.instance.isEnabled);
                                setSheetState(() {});
                              },
                            ),
                            IconButton(
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.close, color: Colors.grey),
                              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(sheetContext),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),

                    if (!kIsWeb && Platform.isAndroid) ...[
                      FutureBuilder<bool>(
                        future: _isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(),
                        builder: (context, snapshot) {
                          final isIgnored = snapshot.data ?? false;
                          return InkWell(
                            onTap: () async {
                              await _requestIgnoreBatteryOptimizations();
                              setSheetState(() {});
                            },
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            child: Container(
                              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: isIgnored ? const Color(0xFF1B3B22) : const Color(0xFF3E2723),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                border: Border.all(
                                  color: isIgnored ? Colors.greenAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.6) : Colors.amberAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.8),
                                  width: 1.2,
                                ),
                              ),
                              child: Row(
                                children: [
                                  Icon(
                                    isIgnored ? Icons.bolt : Icons.battery_alert_rounded,
                                    color: isIgnored ? Colors.greenAccent : Colors.amberAccent,
                                    size: 22,
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 10),
                                  Expanded(
                                    child: Column(
                                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                      children: [
                                        const Text(
                                          '⚡ Робота у фоні без обмежень (Android 17)',
                                          style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 12.5),
                                        ),
                                        const SizedBox(height: 2),
                                        Text(
                                          isIgnored
                                              ? 'Дозволено: надійний радар та сповіщення'
                                              : 'Натисніть для вимкнення обмежень батареї',
                                          style: TextStyle(
                                            color: isIgnored ? Colors.greenAccent.shade100 : Colors.amber.shade200,
                                            fontSize: 10.5,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  Icon(
                                    isIgnored ? Icons.check_circle : Icons.chevron_right,
                                    color: isIgnored ? Colors.greenAccent : Colors.amberAccent,
                                    size: 20,
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        },
                      ),
                    ],

                    if (watchedList.isEmpty)
                      const Padding(
                        padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0),
                        child: Text('Немає доданих зон безпеки', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)),
                      )
                    else
                      ...watchedList.map((loc) {
                        return Container(
                          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: const Color(0xFF2A2A2A),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            border: Border.all(
                              color: loc.enabled ? Colors.purpleAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.4) : Colors.transparent,
                              width: 1,
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: ListTile(
                            dense: true,
                            leading: IconButton(
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.my_location, color: Colors.purpleAccent, size: 20),
                              tooltip: 'Показати на мапі',
                              onPressed: () {
                                mapController.move(loc.point, 13.5);
                                Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                              },
                            ),
                            title: Text(loc.name, style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.white)),
                            subtitle: Text(
                              '${loc.address}\nРадіус: ${(loc.radiusMeters / 1000).toStringAsFixed(1)} км',
                              style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade400, fontSize: 11),
                            ),
                            isThreeLine: loc.address.isNotEmpty,
                            trailing: Row(
                              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                              children: [
                                Switch(
                                  value: loc.enabled,
                                  activeThumbColor: Colors.purpleAccent,
                                  onChanged: (val) {
                                    ref.read(watchedLocationsProvider.notifier).toggleLocation(loc.id);
                                  },
                                ),
                                IconButton(
                                  icon: const Icon(Icons.delete_outline, color: Colors.redAccent, size: 20),
                                  tooltip: 'Видалити',
                                  onPressed: () {
                                    ref.read(watchedLocationsProvider.notifier).removeLocation(loc.id);
                                  },
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                        );
                      }),

                    if (!isAddingNew) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      SizedBox(
                        width: double.infinity,
                        child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.add_location_alt_outlined),
                          label: const Text('Додати нову зону безпеки'),
                          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: Colors.purpleAccent,
                            foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                          ),
                          onPressed: () {
                            setSheetState(() => isAddingNew = true);
                          },
                        ),
                      ),
                    ] else ...[
                      const Divider(color: Colors.grey, height: 24),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          const Text(
                            'Додати нову зону безпеки',
                            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.purpleAccent),
                          ),
                          TextButton.icon(
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.close, size: 16, color: Colors.grey),
                            label: const Text('Скасувати', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)),
                            onPressed: () {
                              setSheetState(() {
                                isAddingNew = false;
                                searchResults = [];
                                selectedResult = null;
                                searchCtrl.clear();
                                nameCtrl.clear();
                              });
                            },
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 10),

                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: TextField(
                              controller: searchCtrl,
                              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                              decoration: InputDecoration(
                                hintText: 'Введіть вулицю або адресу в Києві...',
                                hintStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontSize: 13),
                                prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.search, color: Colors.purpleAccent, size: 20),
                                filled: true,
                                fillColor: const Color(0xFF2A2A2A),
                                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                                border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                              ),
                              onSubmitted: (val) async {
                                if (val.trim().isEmpty) return;
                                setSheetState(() => isSearching = true);
                                final results = await searchStreetAddress(val);
                                setSheetState(() {
                                  isSearching = false;
                                  searchResults = results;
                                });
                              },
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          ElevatedButton(
                            style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                              backgroundColor: Colors.purpleAccent,
                              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
                              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
                            ),
                            onPressed: isSearching ? null : () async {
                              if (searchCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) return;
                              setSheetState(() => isSearching = true);
                              final results = await searchStreetAddress(searchCtrl.text);
                              setSheetState(() {
                                isSearching = false;
                                searchResults = results;
                              });
                            },
                            child: isSearching
                                ? const SizedBox(width: 16, height: 16, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2, color: Colors.white))
                                : const Text('Пошук'),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),

                      if (searchResults.isNotEmpty)
                        Container(
                          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(4),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: const Color(0xFF262626),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                            border: Border.all(color: Colors.purpleAccent.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
                          ),
                          child: Column(
                            children: searchResults.map((res) {
                              return ListTile(
                                dense: true,
                                leading: const Icon(Icons.location_on, color: Colors.purpleAccent, size: 18),
                                title: Text(
                                  res['display_name'].toString(),
                                  style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 12),
                                  maxLines: 2,
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                ),
                                onTap: () {
                                  setSheetState(() {
                                    selectedResult = res;
                                    searchCtrl.text = res['display_name'].toString();
                                    searchResults = [];
                                    if (nameCtrl.text.isEmpty) {
                                      nameCtrl.text = 'Моя зона';
                                    }
                                  });
                                },
                              );
                            }).toList(),
                          ),
                        ),

                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      TextField(
                        controller: nameCtrl,
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                        decoration: InputDecoration(
                          hintText: 'Коротка назва (напр. Дім, Робота, Офіс)',
                          hintStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontSize: 13),
                          prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.bookmark_outline, color: Colors.purpleAccent, size: 20),
                          filled: true,
                          fillColor: const Color(0xFF2A2A2A),
                          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                          border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                        ),
                      ),

                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                        children: [
                          const Text('Радіус тривоги:', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13)),
                          Text(
                            '${radiusKm.toStringAsFixed(1)} км',
                            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.purpleAccent, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      Slider(
                        value: radiusKm,
                        min: 1.0,
                        max: 10.0,
                        divisions: 18,
                        activeColor: Colors.purpleAccent,
                        inactiveColor: Colors.grey.shade700,
                        label: '${radiusKm.toStringAsFixed(1)} км',
                        onChanged: (val) {
                          setSheetState(() => radiusKm = val);
                        },
                      ),

                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.check),
                              label: const Text('Зберегти зону'),
                              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                                backgroundColor: Colors.purpleAccent,
                                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
                                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                              ),
                              onPressed: () {
                                if (selectedResult == null && searchCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty) {
                                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                    const SnackBar(
                                      content: Text(
                                        'Спочатку знайдіть та виберіть адресу!',
                                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  );
                                  return;
                                }
                                final name = nameCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty ? 'Моя зона' : nameCtrl.text.trim();
                                final lat = selectedResult != null ? (selectedResult!['lat'] as double) : 50.4501;
                                final lon = selectedResult != null ? (selectedResult!['lon'] as double) : 30.5234;
                                final address = selectedResult != null ? selectedResult!['display_name'].toString() : searchCtrl.text.trim();

                                final newLoc = WatchedLocation(
                                  id: DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
                                  name: name,
                                  address: address,
                                  point: LatLng(lat, lon),
                                  radiusMeters: radiusKm * 1000.0,
                                  enabled: true,
                                );

                                ref.read(watchedLocationsProvider.notifier).addLocation(newLoc);
                                mapController.move(newLoc.point, 13.5);
                                Navigator.pop(sheetContext);

                                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                  SnackBar(
                                    content: Text(
                                      'Зону "$name" успішно додано!',
                                      style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                                    ),
                                    backgroundColor: Colors.purple,
                                  ),
                                );
                              },
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 8),
                          OutlinedButton(
                            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                              foregroundColor: Colors.grey,
                              side: const BorderSide(color: Colors.grey),
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12, horizontal: 16),
                              shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                            ),
                            onPressed: () {
                              setSheetState(() {
                                isAddingNew = false;
                                searchResults = [];
                                selectedResult = null;
                                searchCtrl.clear();
                                nameCtrl.clear();
                              });
                            },
                            child: const Text('Скасувати'),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );

    searchCtrl.dispose();
    nameCtrl.dispose();
  }

  void showAddPointFromMapDialog({
  required BuildContext context,
  required LatLng point,
  required WidgetRef ref,
  required dynamic watchedLocationsProvider,
}) async {
    final nameCtrl = TextEditingController(text: 'Моя точка');
    double radiusKm = 4.0;

    await showModalBottomSheet(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF1E1E1E),
      shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(20)),
      ),
      builder: (sheetContext) {
        return StatefulBuilder(
          builder: (ctx, setDialogState) {
            return Padding(
              padding: EdgeInsets.only(
                bottom: MediaQuery.of(sheetContext).viewInsets.bottom + 16,
                left: 16,
                right: 16,
                top: 16,
              ),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Center(
                    child: Container(
                      width: 40,
                      height: 4,
                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: Colors.grey.shade600, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(2)),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.add_location_alt, color: Colors.purpleAccent, size: 22),
                      SizedBox(width: 8),
                      Text(
                        'Додати зону з точки на мапі',
                        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, color: Colors.white),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text(
                    'Координати: ${point.latitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}, ${point.longitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}',
                    style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade400, fontSize: 12),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  TextField(
                    controller: nameCtrl,
                    style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 13),
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: 'Назва зони (наприклад, Дача, Склад)',
                      hintStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey.shade500, fontSize: 13),
                      prefixIcon: const Icon(Icons.bookmark_outline, color: Colors.purpleAccent, size: 20),
                      filled: true,
                      fillColor: const Color(0xFF2A2A2A),
                      contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
                      border: OutlineInputBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8), borderSide: BorderSide.none),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      const Text('Радіус тривоги:', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 13)),
                      Text(
                        '${radiusKm.toStringAsFixed(1)} км',
                        style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.purpleAccent, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 14),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  Slider(
                    value: radiusKm,
                    min: 1.0,
                    max: 10.0,
                    divisions: 18,
                    activeColor: Colors.purpleAccent,
                    inactiveColor: Colors.grey.shade700,
                    label: '${radiusKm.toStringAsFixed(1)} км',
                    onChanged: (val) {
                      setDialogState(() => radiusKm = val);
                    },
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  SizedBox(
                    width: double.infinity,
                    child: ElevatedButton.icon(
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.check),
                      label: const Text('Зберегти зону'),
                      style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: Colors.purpleAccent,
                        foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
                        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10)),
                      ),
                      onPressed: () {
                        final name = nameCtrl.text.trim().isEmpty ? 'Точка на карті' : nameCtrl.text.trim();
                        final newLoc = WatchedLocation(
                          id: DateTime.now().millisecondsSinceEpoch.toString(),
                          name: name,
                          address: 'Точка на карті (${point.latitude.toStringAsFixed(3)}, ${point.longitude.toStringAsFixed(3)})',
                          point: point,
                          radiusMeters: radiusKm * 1000.0,
                          enabled: true,
                        );
                        ref.read(watchedLocationsProvider.notifier).addLocation(newLoc);
                        Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                          SnackBar(
                            content: Text(
                              'Зону "$name" успішно додано!',
                              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontWeight: FontWeight.w500),
                            ),
                            backgroundColor: Colors.purple,
                          ),
                        );
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        );
      },
    );

    nameCtrl.dispose();
  }

      "lon": 29.8172
    },
    {
      "stem": "дідівщин",
      "name": "Дідівщина",
      "lat": 50.1472,
      "lon": 29.7741
    },
    {
      "stem": "кожанк",
      "name": "Кожанка",
      "lat": 49.9682,
      "lon": 29.7612
    },
    {
      "stem": "малополовецьк",
      "name": "Малополовецьке",
      "lat": 49.8984,
      "lon": 29.8142
    }
  ]
}

    test('Parses "На Водосховищі один в район Вишгорода" from North to South', () {
      final threats = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats('На Водосховищі один в район Вишгорода');
      expect(threats.isNotEmpty, isTrue);
      final path = threats.first['path'] as List;
      expect(path.length, equals(2));
      // First is Reservoir (50.76, 30.50), second is Vyshhorod (50.584, 30.488)
      expect(path.first['lat'], closeTo(50.7600, 0.01));
      expect(path.first['lon'], closeTo(30.5000, 0.01));
      expect(path.last['lat'], closeTo(50.584, 0.01));
      expect(path.last['lon'], closeTo(30.488, 0.01));
      // North to south: first lat > second lat
      expect(path.first['lat'] > path.last['lat'], isTrue);
    });

    test('Parses "З Черкаської на район Тетіїв" from South to North', () {
      final threats = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats('З Черкаської на район Тетіїв');
      expect(threats.isNotEmpty, isTrue);
      final path = threats.first['path'] as List;
      expect(path.length, equals(2));
      // First is Cherkasy border (49.18, 30.10), second is Tetiyiv (49.3658, 29.69)
      expect(path.first['lat'], closeTo(49.1800, 0.01));
      expect(path.first['lon'], closeTo(30.1000, 0.01));
      expect(path.last['lat'], closeTo(49.3658, 0.01));
      expect(path.last['lon'], closeTo(29.6900, 0.01));
      // South to north: first lat < second lat
      expect(path.first['lat'] < path.last['lat'], isTrue);
    });

  group('Problem 3: Smart Geo-Deduplication', () {
    test('Deduplicates messages from different channels at same coords within 120s and merges channel labels', () {
      final baseTime = DateTime.now().toUtc().subtract(const Duration(minutes: 5));
      final rawMsgs = [
        {
          'id': 101,
          'channel': 'Kyiv AirDefense',
          'text': 'Троєщина',
          'date': baseTime.toIso8601String(),
          'type': 'danger',
        },
        {
          'id': 202,
          'channel': 'SK_DM_SK',
          'text': 'Вилізло одне на Трою',
          'date': baseTime.add(const Duration(seconds: 30)).toIso8601String(),
          'type': 'danger',
        },
      ];

      final deduped = ThreatParser.deduplicateMessages(rawMsgs);
      expect(deduped.length, equals(1));
      expect(deduped.first['channel'], contains('Kyiv AirDefense'));
      expect(deduped.first['channel'], contains('SK_DM_SK'));
    });

    test('Does NOT deduplicate distant targets (> 6 km) even within same minute', () {
      final baseTime = DateTime.now().toUtc().subtract(const Duration(minutes: 5));

        {
          'id': 101,
          'channel': 'Kyiv AirDefense',

          'text': 'Тетіїв шахед',
          'date': baseTime.add(const Duration(seconds: 15)).toIso8601String(),
          'type': 'danger',
        },
      ];

      final deduped = ThreatParser.deduplicateMessages(rawMsgs);
      expect(deduped.length, equals(2));
    });

  group('Release v1.2.24: Speed, Bank Card Filter, Numbered Targets, "Не мінус", External Districts & Gazetteer', () {
    test('Speed extraction and altitude cleaning', () {

      // Speed phrases and 750 kV should NOT be parsed as altitude

      expect(ThreatParser.extractAltitude('700'), equals(700));

    test('Numbered targets pre-splitting assigns orderTag 1 and 2', () {

      expect((threats[1]['path'] as List).first['name'], contains('Згурів'));
    });

    test('"Не мінус" protection and dump clear phrases', () {
      // Negated clear is NOT clear
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('Не мінус, ще літає'), equals('danger'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('ще не зник'), equals('danger'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('не збито'), equals('danger'));

      // Real dump clear phrases
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('все наче'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('ніби все'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('нема його'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('-1'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('ушов назад'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('втрачено над водосховищем'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('попередньо збили'), equals('clear'));
    });

    test('External district hashtag and phrase rejection without Kyiv mention', () {
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('БпЛА в напрямку #харківський_район'), equals('ignore'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('Шахед зайшов в дніпровський район'), equals('ignore'));

  group('Release v1.2.25: Instant Threat Disappearance on Shootdown Reply', () {
    test('"Знищено", "-1", "Збито" are recognized as clear and destruction', () {
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('Знищено'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('збито'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('-1'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('ліквідовано'), equals('clear'));
      expect(ThreatParser.categorizeLocalMessage('мінус 1'), equals('clear'));
    });

    test('Simulate message reply chain: track created, then reply "Знищено" removes target and prevents resurrection', () {
      // Message 1: Initial threat approaching Dymer from south
      final msg1Text = 'Димер з півдня';
      final parsed1 = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats(msg1Text);
      expect(parsed1.isNotEmpty, isTrue);

      final List<Map<String, dynamic>> threats = [
        {
          'msgId': 101,
          'postId': 'kyiv_airdef/45120',
          'lastPostId': 'kyiv_airdef/45120',
          'historyPostIds': {'kyiv_airdef/45120'},
          'historyMsgIds': {101},
          'channel': 'Kyiv AirDefense 🌇',
          'text': msg1Text,
          'route': 'південь -> Димер',
          'path': [const LatLng(50.60, 30.30), const LatLng(50.78, 30.30)],
          'waypoints': [const LatLng(50.60, 30.30), const LatLng(50.78, 30.30)],
          'type': 'shahed_jet',
          'speed': 550.0,
          'lastUpdated': DateTime.parse('2026-10-15T20:03:00Z'),
        }
      ];
      expect(threats.length, equals(1));

      // Message 2: Reply at 20:07 saying "Знищено"
      final msg2 = {
        'id': 102,
        'postId': 'kyiv_airdef/45125',
        'reply_to_post_id': 'kyiv_airdef/45120',
        'reply_quoted_text': 'Димер з півдня',
        'channel': 'Kyiv AirDefense 🌇',
        'text': 'Знищено',
        'date': '2026-10-15T20:07:00Z',
      };

      // Verify matching logic
      final replyToPost = msg2['reply_to_post_id'];
      int targetIdx = -1;
      for (int ti = threats.length - 1; ti >= 0; ti--) {
        if (threats[ti]['lastPostId'] == replyToPost ||
            (threats[ti]['historyPostIds'] as Set).contains(replyToPost)) {
          targetIdx = ti;
          break;
        }
      }
      expect(targetIdx, equals(0));

      final cleared = threats.removeAt(targetIdx);
      cleared['isDestroyed'] = true;
      expect(threats.isEmpty, isTrue);

      final recentlyClearedLocations = <({LatLng point, DateTime time, String? channel})>[];
      final pList = (cleared['waypoints'] as List<LatLng>?) ?? (cleared['path'] as List<LatLng>?) ?? [];
      final msg2Time = DateTime.parse(msg2['date']!.toString());
      for (final pt in pList) {
        recentlyClearedLocations.add((point: pt, time: msg2Time, channel: msg2['channel']?.toString()));
      }
      expect(recentlyClearedLocations.length, equals(2));

      // Attempted delayed message at 20:07:30 from another channel:
      final lateMsgTime = DateTime.parse('2026-10-15T20:07:30Z');
      final latePath = [const LatLng(50.78, 30.30)];
      bool isResurrected = false;
      for (final pt in latePath) {
        for (final clr in recentlyClearedLocations) {
          if (lateMsgTime.difference(clr.time).inSeconds.abs() <= 120) {
            final dMeters = const Distance().as(LengthUnit.Meter, clr.point, pt);
            if (dMeters <= 5000.0) {
              isResurrected = true;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
        if (isResurrected) break;
      }
      expect(isResurrected, isTrue);
    });
  });

  group('Release v1.2.26: Stolitsia Vocative / Header Filter & Multi-Location Split', () {
    test('Vocative header + list of separate targets without Stolitsia target', () {
      final text = '''Столиця наступні 3
Вишгород Гостомель Боря Обухів
І далі по руху обережно''';
      final threats = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats(text);
      expect(threats.length, equals(4));
      expect(threats.any((t) => (t['path'] as List).any((p) => p['name'].toString().contains('Столиц'))), isFalse);
      expect((threats[0]['path'] as List).first['name'], equals('Вишгород'));
      expect((threats[1]['path'] as List).first['name'], equals('Гостомель'));
      expect((threats[2]['path'] as List).first['name'], equals('Бориспіль'));
      expect((threats[3]['path'] as List).first['name'], equals('Обухів'));
    });

    test('Vocative prefix on same line does not produce Stolitsia target', () {
      final text = 'Столиця, увага! 1 на Вишгород';
      final threats = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats(text);
      expect(threats.length, equals(1));
      expect(threats.any((t) => (t['path'] as List).any((p) => p['name'].toString().contains('Столиц'))), isFalse);
      expect((threats[0]['path'] as List).first['name'], equals('Вишгород'));
    });

    test('Directional destination TO capital ("курс на столицю", "в бік столиці") parses Stolitsia', () {
      final text1 = 'Шахед з півночі курс на столицю';
      final threats1 = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats(text1);
      expect(threats1.length, equals(1));
      expect(threats1.any((t) => (t['path'] as List).any((p) => p['name'].toString().contains('Столиц'))), isTrue);

      final text2 = 'Один в бік столиці через Вишгород';
      final threats2 = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats(text2);
      expect(threats2.length, equals(1));
      expect((threats2[0]['path'] as List).length, equals(2));
      expect(threats2.any((t) => (t['path'] as List).any((p) => p['name'].toString().contains('Столиц'))), isTrue);
    });

    test('True trajectory with "через" and "->" is preserved as single path', () {
      final text1 = 'з Вишгорода через Гостомель на Обухів';
      final threats1 = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats(text1);
      expect(threats1.length, equals(1));
      expect((threats1[0]['path'] as List).length, equals(3));

      final text2 = 'Вишгород -> Гостомель -> Обухів';
      final threats2 = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats(text2);
      expect(threats2.length, equals(1));
      expect((threats2[0]['path'] as List).length, equals(3));
    });
  });

  group('Release v1.2.27: Green Shootdown Card & Active Threats Filter', () {
    test('Destroyed threat is kept for 35s for green badge, but excluded from active threats count', () {
      final now = DateTime.parse('2026-10-15T20:10:00Z');
      final shootdownTime = DateTime.parse('2026-10-15T20:09:40Z'); // 20s ago

      List<Map<String, dynamic>> threats = [
        {
          'msgId': 101,
          'path': [const LatLng(50.45, 30.52), const LatLng(50.50, 30.60)],
          'type': 'shahed',
          'isDestroyed': true,
          'destroyedAt': shootdownTime,
          'lastUpdated': shootdownTime,
        },
        {
          'msgId': 102,
          'path': [const LatLng(50.35, 30.40)],
          'type': 'shahed',
          'isDestroyed': false,
          'lastUpdated': now,
        }
      ];

      // Smart TTL filter (at 20s after destruction)
      threats = threats.where((t) {
        if (t['isDestroyed'] == true) {
          final destroyedAt = (t['destroyedAt'] as DateTime?) ?? (t['lastUpdated'] as DateTime?) ?? now;
          return now.difference(destroyedAt).inSeconds.abs() <= 35;
        }
        return true;
      }).toList();

      // Total threats on map includes the destroyed one (for green badge rendering)
      expect(threats.length, equals(2));
      expect(threats.any((t) => t['isDestroyed'] == true), isTrue);

      // Active threats count excludes destroyed threats
      final activeThreats = threats.where((t) => t['isDestroyed'] != true).toList();
      expect(activeThreats.length, equals(1));
      expect(activeThreats.first['msgId'], equals(102));

      // After 36 seconds, the destroyed threat is cleaned up from map
      final futureNow = DateTime.parse('2026-10-15T20:10:16Z'); // 36s after shootdown
      threats = threats.where((t) {
        if (t['isDestroyed'] == true) {
          final destroyedAt = (t['destroyedAt'] as DateTime?) ?? (t['lastUpdated'] as DateTime?) ?? futureNow;
          return futureNow.difference(destroyedAt).inSeconds.abs() <= 35;
        }
        return true;
      }).toList();

      expect(threats.length, equals(1));
      expect(threats.any((t) => t['isDestroyed'] == true), isFalse);
    });

    test('Borshchahivka slang forms ("Борщаги", "на Борщазі") map to residential district and not gas station', () {
      final threats1 = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats('Борщаги');
      expect(threats1.isNotEmpty, isTrue);
      final p1 = (threats1.first['path'] as List).first;
      expect(p1['name'], equals('Борщагівка'));
      expect(p1['lat'], closeTo(50.4280, 0.005));
      expect(p1['lon'], closeTo(30.3780, 0.005));

      final threats2 = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats('Шахед на Борщазі');
      expect(threats2.isNotEmpty, isTrue);
      final p2 = (threats2.first['path'] as List).first;
      expect(p2['name'], equals('Борщагівка'));
      expect(p2['lat'], closeTo(50.4280, 0.005));

      final threats3 = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats('Курс на Південну Борщагівку');
      expect(threats3.isNotEmpty, isTrue);
      final p3 = (threats3.first['path'] as List).first;
      expect(p3['name'], equals('Південна Борщагівка'));

      // Food / restaurant "борщ" is not parsed as an air defense target
      final threatsBorshch = ThreatParser.parseLocalThreats('Зварили смачний борщ');
      expect(threatsBorshch.isEmpty, isTrue);
    });
  });
}
